    u_3} : ∀ {Ω : Type u_1} {Γ : Type u_2} {Δ : Type u_3} [inst : Fintype.{u_1} Ω] [inst_1 : Fintype.{u_2} Γ]

  (p : Fin k) (d : RiffleUpperV2.Exploration.Direction)
  (ω v :
    @RiffleUpperV2.Sample k n
      (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) L
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
      cuts)
  (j : Fin (@RiffleUpperV2.Exploration.pileSize k n L cuts p))
  (hv :
    @RiffleUpperV2.Exploration.historyFiber k n L cuts p d
      (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat)
        (@Fin.val (@RiffleUpperV2.Exploration.pileSize k n L cuts p) j)
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
      ω v)
  (r : Fin (@RiffleUpperV2.Exploration.pileSize k n L cuts p))
  (hjr :
    @LE.le.{0} (Fin (@RiffleUpperV2.Exploration.pileSize k n L cuts p))
      (@instLEFin (@RiffleUpperV2.Exploration.pileSize k n L cuts p)) j r),
  Or
    (@Eq.{1}
      (RiffleUpperV2.Word k
        (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) L
          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1)))))
      (@RiffleUpperV2.Conditional.revealWords k n L cuts p d v r)
      (@RiffleUpperV2.Conditional.revealWords k n L cuts p d ω j))
    (@RiffleUpperV2.Conditional.strictFuture k
      (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) L
